- **S302 found that the missing "Sign in with Obelisk" was never a code bug, and that the promotion path it unblocked is blocked by the identity provider itself.** Last updated: 2026-08-01. Measured, not assumed: live `/vault-member/` carries **0** `href="/login"` links and **4** password fields against the repo's **2** and **0**, loads legacy `supabase-client.js` and **no `identity.js`** at all, while `/login` returns 302 with valid per-request PKCE. The live homepage, `/membership/`, `/join/` and `/vault-wall/` have **zero** `/login` links between them. The button exists and works; it has never been delivered, because `vault-member/` is SENSITIVE-classified and withheld from the content lane — **the lane built to ship safely while identity work was held is structurally incapable of shipping the identity work.** Shipped: provider-side logout, since sign-out previously deleted only our KV record and left the Obelisk grant and its refresh token alive. Revocation runs *before* the delete (that record is the only place the tokens exist) and is non-fatal by construction, so a provider that is down can never trap a user signed in; tests 13 → 21. Building it produced the finding that changed the plan: **Obelisk advertises `revocation_endpoint` and `end_session_endpoint` in its discovery document and implements neither** — implemented routes answer with protocol errors (`invalid_request`, `unsupported_grant_type`, `invalid_token`), these answer 404 `unknown-auth-route`; a form-encoded POST returns 400 `bad-json` while valid JSON returns the 404, so the body is parsed before routing and that 400 is not evidence the route exists. A hermetic suite passed **21/21 against a compliant mock** while the real provider had no such route, so the live probe was the only control that could have caught it. Because `revocation` is one of the five `real-provider-e2e` legs, the promotion approved this session is blocked on the provider rather than on a founder sign-in, and the guidance published one turn earlier was corrected rather than left to age. A 404 is recorded as `not_implemented`, never `failed`, and cached per issuer — `failed` invites a retry on every sign-out, `not_implemented` is a cross-repo finding. Left unshipped and named: the token 400 silent sign-out (a member with a valid edge session is shown a signed-out portal, no retry, no message), console hygiene, and the hold-reason trim.

- **S301 finished the Obelisk identity lane and found the archive it was gating had been throwing for every signed-in member.** Last updated: 2026-08-01. S300 left three blockers on `api/identity-migration-receipt.json` and labelled two of them human-blocked on three absent Supabase credentials. Those credentials are now in the gateway and all four authority planes probe `ready` (REST 200 · management 200 · SQL 201 · functions 200), which made the work agent work under CANON-019/CANON-040 — so **blockers went 3 → 1**, and the survivor is the legitimately founder-gated `real-provider-e2e-pending`. **The audit understated the defect.** Its premise was "the Eternal tier is narrowed out of content it pays for". The behavioural probe found `public.get_classified_files()` **raising SQLSTATE 42702** — `id` ambiguous between the `RETURNS TABLE` out-parameter and `vault_members.id` — for *every* authenticated caller. The classified archive returned nothing to anyone, and the repair had been sitting committed for nine days. Applied via the management API with a pre-image captured first; after: the RPC executes, all three entitlement objects carry `('vault_sparked','vault_sparked_pro')`, anonymous callers still get zero rows, and a rank-8 free member is still correctly denied. The `eternal-intelligence` edge function was **drifted** — proven by byte-searching the deployed ESZIP (38 of 40 transpile-surviving markers present, `GET, POST, OPTIONS` and the staging-origin allowance absent) — and is redeployed at version 3 → 4 with all 40 present. **The root fix is that none of this can be typed any more.** `IDENTITY_MIGRATION_EVIDENCE.json` was hand-authored and fed a public receipt, so two production blockers were clearable by editing prose; `verify-supabase-runtime.mjs` (36 self-tests) and `verify-obelisk-edge-deployment.mjs` (19) are now its only supported writers and write only what they re-read from the provider *after* the write. **Two of the session's own assertions were wrong and were fixed, not weakened.** The first behavioural control asserted "the Eternal subscriber is unlocked" — but the archive gates on rank *and* plan, the sole Eternal subscriber holds rank 2 (1,065 points), and the only gated row needs rank 3, so it was measuring rank and reporting plan; every count is now rank-scoped and an unobservable direction records `null` rather than rounding to pass or fail. The marker extractor's second flaw was caught by its own self-test: a length-filtered quote regex paired the closing quote of one short literal with the opening quote of the next, emitting the phantom marker `" && req.method !== "`. **Capability discovery was itself generating phantom blockers:** `resolveCapability` returned the same empty-`missing` shape for an absent credential and for a name that does not exist, so `--for supabase` (the real entries are `supabase.admin` / `supabase.client`) read MISSING throughout while every Supabase plane was ready — `✗ UNKNOWN` (exit 3, ranked suggestions) is now separate from `⛔ MISSING` (exit 1), gated, and SKIPs rather than passing vacuously when CI cannot reach the map. The receipt also stopped advertising staging: it took the first `OBELISK_REDIRECT_URI` in `wrangler.toml`, and production defines none, so resolution is now environment-scoped with the worker's own `DEFAULTS` as the fallback. Production edge re-verified live rather than trusted from S300's log — per-request `state`/`nonce`/`code_challenge` proven to *differ* across two independent observations, a forged callback rejected with zero `Set-Cookie`, `/api/auth/me` anonymous-null. **Two board facts were wrong and are corrected:** the account count is **252**, not 143; and bulk-linking is not agent-executable at all, because linking needs an `obelisk_sub` only a real sign-in produces — `check-obelisk-link-readiness.mjs` measures the four ways that path fails a login closed instead (0 duplicate-email groups, 0 duplicate-subject groups, 2 accounts with no email — clean), and instruments the login scan cliff (3 admin requests per sign-in; **1,748 accounts of headroom** before `supabase_user_scan_limit` breaks every login). That fix touches the auth flow and is **escalated, not applied**. Found while deploying: the Eternal Dispatch briefing rendered `SEALED` in a FORGE/SPARKED/**VAULTED** lifecycle triple, conflating seven unannounced projects with a lifecycle state on a paying member's surface.

- **S300 found production serving a six-day-old build behind an interlock that read green, and made that class of silence impossible.** Last updated: 2026-07-31. Every signal was green: `pages-deploy` runs all reported *success*, because held runs are source-publication receipts rather than deploys. Verified by direct probe, not inference — live `/api/build-sha.json` returned `4a72961d` (`deployedBy: pages-deploy-content-hotfix`, 2026-07-26) while repo `index.html` referenced `style.shell-0bcf6496a0.css` against production's `style.shell-86cb6a57c2.css`. **424 commits / 7.1 days** behind by session end. Root cause traced end to end: the interlock gates *all* promotion, its five hold reasons are entirely identity-shaped, and they resolve to three Supabase credentials genuinely absent from the gateway (name-only search per CANON-019 — **not** a phantom blocker). **Three defects produced the silence, all fixed.** (1) Retention never expired: `build-deploy-currency` correctly retains the last usable observation across a Cloudflare bot-challenge, but with no ceiling a permanently-challenged vantage becomes a frozen gauge still rendered as a measurement; past `OBSERVATION_MAX_AGE_HOURS` the state is now `unverified`, checked **before** `current` so a stale zero-drift reading cannot certify production either, with retention age frozen from the two observation stamps (never wall-clock) so `--check` stays byte-stable. (2) Nothing read the measurement: `check-deploy-currency-gate.mjs` is that alarm, deliberately a **separate program** from the reading — the prober can be challenged or offline, this gate reads only the committed receipt and can always fire, *including because the reading aged out*; doctor went 13/15-all-clear to **13/16 with 1 blocking**. (3) The canon named an owner that checks something else: `CANON_MATRIX` marks CANON-036 doctor-owned via a studio-ops probe that verifies each project *declares* a deploy-currency strategy — registry metadata, not whether any production is current — so conformance read `0 GAP / vector green` throughout; the new probe is `deploy-currency-live` so two different questions never share an id. `check-canon-ownership-reachable.mjs` generalises the detectable half and found **4 phantom probe owners (CANON-012/018/023/024, three ABSOLUTE-tier)** reporting `doctor-owned` while no such probe exists in any registry — sibling-owned data, so warn + Ark `pattern-share`, never a cross-repo edit. **Release path:** an auto-scoped content lane now promotes content-pure paths and withholds the rest at the deployed baseline. The audit proposed all-or-nothing purity; run against the real backlog that was dead on arrival (206/529 paths legitimately touch `.github/`, `supabase/`, `auth/`), and a gate that can never pass is indistinguishable from no gate — corrected to a **partition** (211 promotable / 321 withheld), fed through the hotfix gate's `--baseline` reference resolution because skipping that is how the S294 hotfix shipped a 404. It has its **own** `confirm_content` dispatch input: no hold released, nothing dispatched, the flip left to the founder. **Three corrections to the session's own audit, recorded not quietly downgraded:** item 4 severity overstated (the nine feeds return honest **404s**, not 200+HTML — inferred from reading bodies without checking status); item 2 mechanism wrong (the probe exists, it checks a proxy); item 1 design wrong (all-or-nothing → partition). **Two pre-existing defects found by building, not by the audit sweep:** production publishes the entire git-tracked tree (`/.cache/ark-inbox.json`, `/context/PROJECT_STATUS.json`, `/logs/WORK_LOG.md` all serve **200**), and `agents.json` sits in a build dependency cycle with no converging order (the reorder was tried, proved equivalent, and reverted). **Two self-inflicted regressions, both caught and fixed:** CI reddened on `agents.json` drift (that cycle), and the uptime cron reddened because a new receipt field was added to the emitter without restoring it on read — it passed every local run because only a *challenged* vantage populates it, reproduced deliberately with an unreachable origin; the first round-trip test was worthless because it hand-mirrored the mapping and stayed green when the fix was deleted, so the mapping was exported and the assertion mutation-verified. `check-public-note-freshness` also rejected this session's own `publicNote` for dev jargon on a visitor-facing field. Verification: `build:check` **261/261 EXIT 0** (was 257 — four gates added), Lighthouse CI green, all workflows green on the pushed tip. Waves C–E deliberately not started: building the content lane revealed the membership/member/vault-wall surfaces are in the shared `SENSITIVE` list *because they render entitlement*, making those consolidations auth-adjacent **and** unable to ride the lane — with production held they would ship to nobody.
- **S299 ran the full agent-neutral `/arc` as one continuous mission and closed the S298 handoff's top next-step: the served deploy-history ledger is now independently compared, not just validated locally.** Last updated: 2026-07-30. `check-staging-deploy-receipt.mjs --remote` previously fetched only the head *receipt*; the chronology ledger (`data/staging-deploy-history.ndjson`, served at `/data/...`) was validated locally. S299 fetches the served ledger over bounded HTTPS, re-validates the chain from scratch, and requires served depth + head + **canonical-content digest** to match a new reproducible anchor `api/staging-deploy-continuity.json` (depth · genesis · head lineage · SHA-256 of the canonical render · source-derived `generatedAt`, so it is byte-stable across rebuilds). The anchor is deliberately excluded from the candidate manifest's CORE_PATHS, so publishing it can never move `candidateRoot` — the receipt/manifest cycle is impossible by construction, asserted by a self-test. 12 continuity self-tests wired into the receipt checker (now **26/26**); live `--remote` proof: `served ledger verified (depth 27 · 11776aea3ce1)`. - **S285 ran the full `/arc` as one continuous mission and root-fixed an observability-honesty defect the S284 board never surfaced: the CI Status Beacon was painting itself RED on GitHub's own transient HTTP 503 — a health beacon reporting the repo unhealthy on the provider's weather.** Last updated: 2026-07-17. `build:check` **215/215 EXIT 0** (orchestrator, direct exit code), doctor **15/15 blockingFailing 0**, unit green, security scan **0 findings**. **/audit was honest about a thin board:** S284 cleared most work, so the genius list was 3 self-referential VERIFY carries — verified against LIVE, not trusted: the Franchise Architect **301 is live** (`/games/vaultspark-football-gm/` → 301 → `/games/franchise-architect/`, new slug 200) and the S282 verify was a stale run (pruned) — both recorded as resolved, not phantom-carried. **Beacon root-fix (D-S285.1):** `build-ci-status-beacon.mjs`'s `gh api` call had no retry and no degrade — a transient 503 threw and exited 1, reddening the `workflow_run` beacon. SIL 997.)
- **S239 diagnosed and fixed a P0 production outage in a single session: the homepage hung indefinitely after every cache purge.** Root cause: the Worker's nonce-injection path called `finalResponse.clone()` twice on a streaming HTMLRewriter output, creating a double-tee chain where both `ctx.waitUntil(cache.put())` readers blocked each other's backpressure — hanging the response forever. The S176 DR-cache feature added the second clone; S238's Pages deploy triggered a `purge_everything` that exposed the bug by clearing the Worker cache and forcing every HTML request through the uncached path. Fix: `await rewriter.transform(upstream).arrayBuffer()` materializes the 136KB HTML body so all `clone()` calls copy an ArrayBuffer reference (not stream tees), safe to call any number of times. Deployed as Worker commit `c2bbcc7a`; smoke-live confirmed: edge / — HTTP 200 in 93ms. Three second-order innovations followed: (1) **OG-coverage observability feed** — `scripts/build-og-coverage.mjs` writes `api/og-coverage.json` on every build (108/108 carded, 42 dark, coverageRatio 1.0), registered in SURFACES with maxDays:2/blockDays:4 so a dead generator blocks the next deploy; added 100% coverage floor warning; self-test 6/6; wired into check-proof-surface.mjs. (2) **Worker rewriter safety gate** — `scripts/check-worker-rewriter-safety.mjs` scans `security-headers-worker.js` for any `.transform(` call not chained with `.arrayBuffer()` — makes the P0 regression impossible to sneak back; self-test 5/5; wired into check-proof-surface.mjs. (3) **Post-purge edge liveness gate** — `smoke-live.mjs --edge-only` (5s timeout × 2 retries, fast 265ms pass) added to `pages-deploy.yml` after the cache purge step; catches the hang class within 15s of every deploy before visitors see it. Genius list verified exhausted: VideoGame JSON-LD complete (S236), OG uniqueness 0 (S238), blockDays generalized (S231), INP data-blocked (0 samples), Forge Window + changelog founder-gated.

Rewired `track()` to the live `/v/rum` transport under a bounded `funnel:` family (`prefixAllowlist('funnel', {charset:[a-z0-9_], maxLen:48})` in the Worker), folded `funnelCtas` into `rollup-rum-ux`, kept `check-rum-allowlist` in sync. **Privacy upgrade:** the old gtag path leaked internal intent enums (`vault_trust`, `journey_stage`) to Google; the new path transmits only the allowlisted event name. Also purged the dead `googletagmanager`/`google-analytics` resource hints (gtag-removal debris) from ~80 page heads (a deterministic git-tracked sweep reached the 2 propagate-skipped pages too) + deduped pre-existing duplicate `js.stripe.com` hints; Stripe (live revenue path) preserved. **Subsumes the open HERO-PLAY-VS-EXPLORE-CTR carry.** (2) **og-image-raster-fix (🔥)** — the `/_og/` Worker returns `image/svg+xml`, and its own source comment falsely claimed "social platforms all rasterize SVG og:image fine" — they don't (FB/X/LinkedIn/Discord/Slack/iMessage all reject SVG for security). So **73 pages' primary `og:image` rendered blank on every platform** (65 saved on Twitter only by a static PNG `twitter:image`; 8 blank everywhere) — every shared link a dead grey rectangle on a site whose growth thesis is "shared links convert." Repointed all 73 to static PNGs (64 reuse the page's twitter PNG, 2 live football titles got bespoke cards, 7 default to `og-image.png`) + shipped `scripts/check-og-images.mjs` (6/6; ERRORs on SVG/`/_og/`/missing-asset share images) folded into `check-proof-surface` (zero build:check length — chain at 7930 of the cmd.exe 8191 cap). Deliberately sequenced AFTER #2 (a share button that posts a blank card is worse than none). (4) **videogame-schema-gate (💡)** — new `scripts/check-videogame-schema.mjs` (5/5) hard-fails if any of the 10 VideoGame pages reintroduces `aggregateRating` without a real review source; confirms the S193 fabricated-rating removal held (0 errors, 21 advisory enrichment warns on FORGE games); folded into `check-proof-surface`. (5) **acquisition-source-breakdown (💡)** — `analytics.js` (loads on 81 pages, already cleans the referrer + respects consent/DNT) now classifies the referrer into a bounded `source:<bucket>` channel (search/social/direct/referral, domain-only, never a URL) emitted once per session to `/v/rum`; Worker `source:` family + `sources` rollup block. The one number a traffic-starved site never measured: which channel the trickle arrives through. **All three new dynamic RUM families got worker-unit coverage** (`worker.unit` 23→25, +2 S194 tests proving free-text/PII/URLs are dropped at the edge). `build:check` **EXIT 0 end-to-end** (115-page crawl, 0 status failures, 0 blocking-script findings); pre-existing untracked `obelisk-passport/` WIP dir parked (not in git HEAD → CI green). (2) **proof-feed-generator-gate** — new `scripts/check-proof-feed-generators.mjs` (12/12) imports the FEEDS list from `build-status-proof.mjs` and fails build:check if any bundled proof feed's `generatedBy` is a hand-seed or missing — converting the S191 memory lesson into a structural gate so "seeded, not generated" is permanently impossible. Building it CAUGHT a real gap: `ci-status.json` had no `generatedBy` at all → stamped it + the `ci-status-beacon.yml` workflow now emits it. 10 bundled feeds confirmed live-derived. (3) **bounded-prefix-allowlist-primitive** — `prefixAllowlist(family,{charset,maxLen})` + `makeRumUxCleaner(exactSet,dynamicMatchers)` added to `worker-lib.mjs` (the unit-tested single source of truth); the Worker now builds `cleanRumUxEvent` from them so a bounded dynamic RUM family (`oracle-answer:helpful:<id>`) is admitted at the edge without loosening the global exact-match Set. Added 2 `worker.unit.spec.js` cases — the RUM sanitizer finally has coverage (23/23 total). (4) **oracle-per-cluster-feedback-finish** — closed the S191 deferred item now that #3 unblocked it: `rollup-rum-ux.mjs` gains `parseOracleAnswer` + a prefix-aware GLOBAL fold (per-cluster names roll into the global helpful-rate) + per-(clusterKey,day) feedback rows with a `*` global aggregate (19/19); the frontend `ignis-answer-engine.js` emits `oracle-answer:<part>:<clusterId>` when a chip identifies the cluster (bounded `clusterSlug`), global fallback for typed queries — the studio now learns WHICH answer clusters miss, not just the global rate; `check-rum-allowlist` in sync. (5) **staging-health-self-refresh** — `check-staging-parity.mjs` made resilient (8s timeout, never throws on an unreachable origin) + a pure `classifyStatus` helper + an honest `staging-unreachable` status with a `reason` + a `--refresh` scheduled mode (6/6); wired a low-churn refresh into `uptime-probe.yml` (piggybacked on the uptime commit cadence, under the 84h seed-rot bound). - **S191 ran the full goal-chain with a deliberately small, ground-truth-verified frontier audit. Theme: COMPLETE THE PROOF SURFACE + HARDEN ITS HONESTY.** The funnel S186-S190 built is data-starved (1 event / 30d, honest-dark) — a traffic problem, not a code problem — so the audit added NO new measurement and instead closed real integration/freshness/WCAG gaps the apparatus left open. Ground-truth first: probed pages.dev (S190 live, generatedAt today), Read-verified that a grep artifact (`\v\rum`) was NOT a bug (the file correctly emits `/v/rum`), and found the status-proof manifest's own feeds were rotting. **Shipped 4:** (1) **reduced-motion-animation-guard** — S190's animated session count-up (`honest-traction-scoreboard.js`) + `vault-rank-bar.js` ran `requestAnimationFrame`/CSS-transition animations with ZERO `prefers-reduced-motion` guard while 40 sibling assets honored it; added a JS guard (jump to final value) + a `@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)` transition-kill — a real WCAG 2.3.3 gap closed. (2) **structured-citation-endpoint** — new `scripts/build-citation.mjs` (9/9 self-test) publishes `api/citation.json`: studio identity + proprietary license (CANON-008) + 4 CONFIRMED, sourced, dated claims (portfolio counts, 190 sessions-in-the-open, the −53% `/` LCP field-win, trustScore 82 + 100% uptime) each linking the public feed that proves it, plus a ready-to-quote `suggestedCitation`. Honest: a claim is emitted only when its source confirms it. Discoverable via `agents.json` discovery block + a Citation line in `llms.txt`. Extends the machine-SEO spine to the citation layer so LLMs cite VaultSpark accurately instead of hallucinating. - **S182 began with a real full outage: the founder reported the site "not loading," and it genuinely was down.** Root cause: the Cloudflare Worker (`cloudflare/security-headers-worker.js`) fetched its own apex route as origin and hung — after the S175 Pages migration the apex had no backing origin, so `fetch(request)` self-looped with zero response. Pages origin (`pages.dev`) was healthy throughout; only the edge Worker was broken. Fixes shipped: (1) `originFetch` now rewrites the primary fetch to the Pages origin **by hostname** (`PRIMARY_ORIGIN`), so it can never loop through its own route; (2) `package.json` `deploy` now defaults to `--env production --config cloudflare/wrangler.toml` (the prior bare `wrangler deploy` was a latent foot-gun); (3) new `scripts/smoke-live.mjs` post-deploy liveness gate wired into `cloudflare-worker-deploy.yml` — a deploy that hangs/5xxs now turns CI RED instead of green-while-down, and (S182 implement) **auto-rolls-back** to the last-good Worker version on a failed gate. - Date: 2026-06-07 (Session 177 — goal-chain: uptime-probe false-alarm root-cause + real-availability rewrite + Worker origin-hang hardening)
- **S177 ran the full `/start → /audit → /implement → /closeout` chain. The seed was S176's `UPTIME-PROBE-VERIFY` item — on inspection the probe's first and only cron run had FAILED and emailed the founder "5 routes failing." Deep live forensics proved it was a false alarm: the site was up the whole time.** Root cause: `vaultsparkstudios.com` **HTML navigation is bot-challenged at the Cloudflare edge** — a browser-UA `text/html` request from a datacenter IP (GitHub Actions, curl, headless) is intercepted *before the Worker* and hangs/403s, while a real residential browser solves the JS clearance transparently. Proof chain: the Pages origin (`vaultsparkstudios-website.pages.dev`) serves every route 200; `wrangler tail` showed scanner-UA + JSON requests reaching the Worker but the browser-UA HTML request **never logging an invocation**; the CF Pages API showed deploy `171c7bd0` "deploy success". The S176 probe measured "can a datacenter IP pass the bot gauntlet," not "is the site up" — a 100%-false-positive monitor, worse than none. **Fixes (2/2 implement items):** (1) `scripts/probe-uptime.mjs` rewritten to schemaVersion 2.0 two-signal model — Pages-origin content availability (unchallenged) + a production JSON liveness path (DNS+CF+Worker chain); the custom-domain HTML status is kept as a *non-alerting informational* field; alerts fire only on real origin/liveness failure. Run dropped 4m14s → ~2s, self-test 10/10. (2) `cloudflare/security-headers-worker.js` `originFetch` hardened with `AbortSignal.timeout(8s)` on the idempotent primary + fallback fetch, so an origin **hang** (the most common real outage — and the shape behind S176's DR layer, which only fired on a clean 5xx) fast-fails into the existing pages.dev failover → DR cache instead of blocking the Worker. Deployed `--env production` (version `bb9a734d`); post-deploy verified scanner-403 + JSON-200 + probe `overall=up`. Captured as DECISIONS 2026-06-07 + agent memory so the next agent does not re-diagnose "site down" (this session nearly did). Verification: `npm run build:check` green end-to-end (108-page crawl, 0 failures); generated artifacts (llms-full-shards, oracle ecosystem-state) settled. Honest caveats: loosening the bot-challenge for unverified crawlers is a zone security-posture call (API token lacks zone-settings scope) — flagged, not changed; verified bots are CF-allowlisted so SEO/social impact is expected minimal. Deferred evidence-gated carries unchanged (TT re-probe ~06-12 · origin field verdict ≥5 samples/side · geo non-US · founder vaultsparked-proof + nav-sheet).

- Previous posture (Session 132): **Founder-driven mobile drawer overhaul. Three iPhone 11 regressions reported with screenshots, each root-caused and shipped end-to-end. (1) Hero "VAULTSPARK STUDIOS" wrapped mid-word ("VAULTSPAR / K") on iPhone 11 portrait — `.forge-line-1` clamp `13vw` overflowed 10 Georgia letters past safe-area at 414px; new ≤480px clamp `(2.4rem, 10.5vw, 3.6rem)` + line-2 `(1.5rem, 6.8vw, 2.4rem)` fits cleanly. (2) Mobile drawer "nothing clickable, nothing opens sub-menus" — root cause was a **stacking-context trap**: `.site-header { position: sticky; z-index: 100 }` creates a stacking context that bounds any fixed descendant at z:100 in document order; `.nav-center.open` (z:200) lived inside the header → trapped; `#nav-backdrop` (body-attached, z:199) rendered ABOVE the entire header stacking context and swallowed every tap on the drawer. Fix in `assets/nav-toggle.js`: portal `nav-menu` to `document.body` on `openMenu()`, restore to original parent on `closeMenu()` via stored `navHome`/`navHomeNext` refs. Drawer + backdrop now siblings in root stacking context, z-index works as declared. Desktop nav unaffected (only portals when drawer opens; restores immediately on close). (3) Drawer text rendered dim grey, looking disabled — specificity trap: theme selectors `body.dark-mode .nav-center a` (0,2,2) outranked the S130 mobile fix `.nav-center.open a` (0,2,1). Fix: `body .nav-center.open a { color: var(--text) !important }` matches specificity (0,2,2) + `!important` belt-and-suspenders. (4) Quality bonuses same pass: iOS-safe scroll lock (`position:fixed; top:-scrollY` replaces `body{overflow:hidden}` which swallows fixed-overlay taps on iOS Safari); drawer palette parity `--mobile-nav-bg` `rgba(0,0,0,0.98)` → `rgba(20,22,32,0.985)` so it reads as a panel against ambient gradients instead of a flat black void. Three commits pushed end-to-session: `8db35ec3` (wordmark squeeze + iOS scroll lock + palette parity) · `d94df39c` (hero clamp + drawer contrast specificity) · `f7f0b7b4` (portal-out-of-header — the real durable fix). Shell rebuilt 3× with orphan cleanup each rotation; final `style.shell-eb829ae758.css` + `nav-toggle.shell-96581b1d55.js`. New memory `feedback_theme_selector_specificity.md` codifies the (0,2,2) vs (0,2,1) trap class for future state-override rules. Founder visually confirmed hero fix; mobile drawer fix pending iPhone re-verify (the durable portal-to-body fix was only landed in the third commit).**

- Previous posture (S122): **Session 122 was a critical login-blockage root cause fix. Founder reported being unable to log in for weeks. Root cause identified from browser dev log: S120's nonce CSP migration (`NONCE_CSP_ENABLED="1"`) introduced `buildCspWithNonce()` which stripped ALL sha256 hashes from `script-src`, replacing them with nonce + `'strict-dynamic'`. This broke Cloudflare Turnstile: the widget creates `about:srcdoc` iframes with inline scripts that inherit the parent page's CSP but cannot receive a nonce injection (they're created dynamically by Cloudflare's JS, not our HTML). Without a matching hash, the inline script is blocked → `_onToken` callback never fires → `VSTurnstile.getToken()` hangs indefinitely → the login form submit never reaches Supabase → user stuck forever. Fix: (1) Added Turnstile's srcdoc inline script hash `sha256-eJGI0Ik4oYe/PKLDOt4wcN76wYs8h+Ew05pMzdY6xG8=` to `SCRIPT_HASHES` in `config/csp-policy.mjs`; (2) Removed hash-stripping from `buildCspWithNonce()` in `cloudflare/security-headers-worker.js` — hashes now preserved alongside nonce, covering any inline scripts that cannot receive a nonce. Worker deploy pending founder action (`npx wrangler deploy --config cloudflare/wrangler.toml --env production`).**

- Previous posture (S121): **Session 121 was a deep login-flow audit + fix pass. Founder reported persistent login issues with Turnstile NaN console spam + "preloaded but not used" warnings on `challenges.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/challenge-platform/h/g/cmg/1`. Root cause: `getToken()` called `turnstile.remove(_widgetId)` then `render()` on every token request — destroying the widget mid-lifecycle orphaned its preloaded challenge resources and produced the NaN spam during widget teardown/re-init. Full rewrite of `assets/turnstile.js`: single-widget lifecycle (never `remove()`), shared `_onToken`/`_onError`/`_onExpired` callbacks, pending-resolvers array for concurrent callers, `reset()` on existing widget instead of destroy+re-render, background reset after serving cached token so next call hits cache too. Separately ran a comprehensive auth-flow audit (Explore subagent, 26 issues found), then applied the highest-impact actionable fixes: (1) `_mapAuthError()` helper in `portal-auth.js` maps all known Supabase v1/v2 error codes (invalid_credential, email_not_confirmed, rate limits, captcha, user_banned, weak_password, User already registered, duplicate username, network errors) to plain-English user messages — raw Supabase codes no longer reach the user; (2) all three auth forms (login, register, forgot) now clear both `.show` class AND `textContent` on each submit so old errors never persist into a retry; (3) `switchTab()` in `portal-core.js` clears all `#auth-view .form-error` elements when switching tabs — stale errors from previous panel no longer bleed through; (4) `onAuthStateChange` in `portal-settings.js` now handles `SIGNED_OUT` event — expired or revoked sessions redirect to login instead of leaving the user stuck on a broken dashboard. 4 files changed, 0 regressions.**

- Previous posture (S120): **Session 120 was a compound sprint: (1) login-system repair pass fixing three cascaded bugs surfaced in the dev log — Turnstile `size:'invisible'` was not a valid parameter (valid: normal/compact/flexible); removing it + replacing with `appearance:'interaction-only'` fixed Turnstile Error 300030 (widget hung) and TrustedTypes blocks in the Turnstile iframe; added 4-min token cache + pre-render on `__vsTurnstileReady` callback for zero-friction login. (2) CSP blocking GTM inline script — hash `sha256-YDBc0l4e7MoGJMuzaifAmfTbiM7yz8H4VUdl1WAOklU=` was commented in `config/csp-policy.mjs` but not added to the array; added to both csp-policy.mjs and the meta CSP tag in vault-member/index.html. (3) IndexedDB member cache — new `vault-member/portal-cache.js` pre-renders dashboard from cache on return visits (instant UX, ~0ms vs ~400ms Supabase RPC) then syncs fresh data in background; integrated into portal-auth.js (write on login), portal-settings.js (pre-render before RPC + write on fresh data), portal-core.js (clear on logout). (6) **NONCE CSP MIGRATION (complete)** — stripped all 109 meta CSP tags from HTML files via `scripts/strip-meta-csp.mjs`; added `MetaCspStripper` HTMLRewriter class to Cloudflare Worker (removes any remaining meta CSP at the edge); disabled `scripts/propagate-csp.mjs` with a process.exit(1) guard to prevent re-addition; `NONCE_CSP_ENABLED="1"` was already set in wrangler.toml; deployed Worker to production (version `1c069071-81c9-46c9-9e8f-1294e263cffb`, routes: vaultsparkstudios.com/* + hub.vaultsparkstudios.com/*). Browser now enforces only the per-request nonce-based HTTP header CSP — the meta hash-based CSP conflict is permanently eliminated. 3 commits pushed: `535ed02` (login bugs + cache + kudos) · `e953dd2` (PROJECT_STATUS) · `f3193f7` (nonce migration + Worker deploy).**

- Previous posture (S81): **CI-plumbing cleanup session** — 5 workflow fixes across 5 YAML files resolved three chronic failures (Accessibility, Lighthouse, E2E compliance), one S80-only regression (Sitemap), and one latent failure (playwright-axe npm ci lockfile). The key architectural move was retiring `sw-version.yml`'s on-push trigger — it had been fighting `scripts/build-shell-assets.mjs` (S77) over who owns `sw.js`'s CACHE_NAME, and that fight was the root cause of the E2E compliance drift. The fingerprinted shell pipeline is now the single owner; sw-version is kept as `workflow_dispatch`-only with a deprecation note until confirmed unused for ≥ 5 sessions. **S80 posture still applies:** full 10-dimension site audit (77/100) + 28-item Tier 1–4 plan captured in TASK_BOARD; `/ignis/` narrative page live; homepage Public Operating Surface relocated to `/studio-pulse/` + `/ignis/`. The "Public Operating Surface" internal-ops block has been removed from the homepage and replaced with a Studio Pulse + IGNIS teaser — Studio OS internals no longer leak to the marketing surface. The new `/ignis/` explainer page converts the opaque IGNIS number into a narrative/brand signal with a live gauge, four-tier scale, five pillars, and a "why we publish it" argument. The vault-proof stat region is now screen-reader live; the pathways section has a noscript fallback; the games catalog now supports shareable/refresh-safe URL filter state; and IGNIS is now site-wide in the Studio footer column (78 pages). The 28-item master plan is captured in TASK_BOARD and memory; infrastructure items (CSP nonce migration, edge-gating the private portals, rate-limit/CSRF on forms) remain HAR-blocked on `CF_WORKER_API_TOKEN`.

## What exists

### Live systems
- **Vault Member portal** (`vault-member/`) — 9-tier rank system, achievements, challenges, Discord role sync, onboarding tour, light-mode phase 2
- **VaultSparked membership** (`vaultsparked/`) — Stripe $4.99–$99.99/mo, 6 price IDs, phase progress bar, gift checkout modal (gift price `price_1TJ7xbGMN60PfJYsPCs5wUUz` set S37)
- **Investor portal** (`investor/`) — gated
- **Universe** — DreadSpike (pivoted to Novel Saga; **S53: Signal Log entry added** — intercepted transmission card with lore fragment), Voidfall full lore page (`/universe/voidfall/`) — Transmission Archive (3 fragments), The Signal, Known Entities (**S53: atmospheric entity 4 hint added** below The Crossed row), Saga meta, Chapter I excerpt (S50)
- **Studio Hub** (`studio-hub/`) — synced from vaultspark-studio-hub repo
- **10 journal posts** — fireView() consent-gated
- **8 game pages** — FORGE/SPARKED/VAULTED radial glow, data-status attrs; status badges correctly positioned (direct child of .hero-art)
- **12 project pages** — status badges fixed (S36): moved outside .hero-art-content to prevent absolute-positioning overlap with h1
- **Compliance pages** — /cookies/, /accessibility/, /rights/ (technology attributions + IP notice; /open-source/ redirects here), /faq/, /careers/, /data-deletion/, /security/, sitemap
- **Members directory** (`members/`) — S58: fixed profile-loading regression from CSP-blocked inline directory script; runtime moved to `/assets/members-directory.js`, clear-filter action uses event delegation, query supports current `vault_points`/`rank_title` plus legacy `points` fallback; "Founding Member" label updated to "Genesis Member".

### Infrastructure
- **Fingerprinted shell asset pipeline** (`scripts/build-shell-assets.mjs`, `assets/shell-manifest.json`, generated `assets/*.shell-*.{css,js}` files, all HTML entrypoints) — Session 77 moved the shared shell layer onto one generated manifest. `style.css`, `theme-toggle.js`, `nav-toggle.js`, and the new `shell-health.js` are fingerprinted, every HTML page is rewritten to those release URLs, old generated variants are cleaned automatically, and `npm run build` / `npm run build:check` now treat shell drift as a build failure instead of an intermittent browser mystery.
- **Homepage shell health monitor** (`assets/shell-health.js`, `index.html`) — Session 77 added a focused runtime guard around the homepage shell. It verifies that the brand/header, hero heading, fingerprinted stylesheet, and forge-letter reveal all resolve correctly, force-reveals the letters if they get stuck hidden, and emits a public-safe `shell_health_issue` analytics event when the homepage shell falls into a bad state. Session 78 audited the monitor and added per-session dedupe plus an explicit `window.VSShellHealth.status = 'ok'` path so the monitor is less noisy and easier to interrogate.
- **Homepage shell regression gate** (`tests/homepage-hero-regression.spec.js`, `scripts/run-local-browser-verify.mjs`, `scripts/run-live-browser-verify.mjs`, `scripts/release-confidence.mjs`, `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`) — Session 77 made the header/hero shell a dedicated browser gate across local and live verification. The spec asserts visible brand/title state, fingerprinted stylesheet attachment, no page errors, and no failed same-origin shell asset requests; the live verify wrapper is now Windows-safe for local execution; release-confidence and CI now treat that shell contract as a real delivery surface. **Post-push result:** homepage shell verify passed against both production and staging on 2026-04-16. **Session 78 result:** the full extended local Chromium browser suite passed `86/86` using the lower-worker runner defaults.
- **Service-worker shell hardening** (`sw.js`) — Session 77 stopped treating mutable shell source files as cacheable release assets. The Worker now caches only the generated fingerprinted shell URLs, bypasses mutable shell source paths, and derives the shell cache identity from the same release fingerprints so HTML/CSS/JS/service-worker revisions cannot drift independently.
- **Micro-feedback engine** (`assets/micro-feedback.js`, `index.html`, `membership/index.html`, `vaultsparked/index.html`, `join/index.html`, `invite/index.html`, `studio-pulse/index.html`) — Session 76 added a shared browser-local feedback surface for user goal, blocker, and usefulness capture across the highest-leverage public pages. It stores public-safe responses locally, renders live summaries back into the site, and gives the website a real direct feedback loop instead of relying only on inferred telemetry.
- **Feedback-aware intelligence bridge** (`assets/public-intelligence.js`, `assets/telemetry-matrix.js`, `assets/trust-depth.js`, `scripts/generate-public-intelligence.mjs`, `scripts/lib/public-intelligence-contracts.mjs`, `context/contracts/*.json`, `api/public-intelligence.json`) — Session 76 extended the generated public-intelligence/contracts spine so local feedback summaries can enrich runtime trust/telemetry reads and remain portable to Studio Hub/social-dashboard surfaces without exposing private data.
- **Shared visitor-state spine** (`assets/intent-state.js`, `assets/pathways-router.js`, `assets/adaptive-cta.js`, `assets/related-content.js`, `assets/funnel-tracking.js`) — Session 75 introduced one shared intent runtime (`intent`, `confidence`, `journey_stage`, `world_affinity`, `trust_level`, `membership_temperature`, `returning_status`) and rewired the existing pathways, CTA, related-rail, and funnel surfaces to consume the same state instead of maintaining separate local logic.
- **World-affinity inference + world-gravity rails** (`assets/intent-state.js`, `assets/related-content.js`, `games/vaultfront/index.html`, `games/solara/index.html`, `games/mindframe/index.html`, `games/the-exodus/index.html`, `universe/voidfall/index.html`, `universe/dreadspike/index.html`, `tests/intelligence-surfaces.spec.js`) — Session 79 deepened the cohesion layer so world affinity is inferred at the per-world/page level (`vaultfront`, `solara`, `mindframe`, `the-exodus`, `voidfall`, `dreadspike`) instead of only generic `player`/`lore` intent. Related rails now render directly on those game/universe pages, the rails adapt their headings by context, and the intelligence-surface browser spec now verifies those new routes instead of only the core conversion pages.
- **Adaptive narrative personalization** (`assets/adaptive-cta.js`, `assets/pathways-router.js`, `assets/network-spine.js`) — Session 76 expanded the shared guidance layer so copy emphasis and next-move framing now react more explicitly to hesitation states such as `need_proof`, `price_unsure`, and `want_gameplay`, plus progress-tracking membership intent.
- **Visitor pathways layer** (`assets/pathways-router.js`, `assets/adaptive-cta.js`, `index.html`, `membership/index.html`, `vaultsparked/index.html`, `join/index.html`, `invite/index.html`) — Session 74 shipped a constrained intent router for player / member / supporter / investor / lore-seeker paths. It remembers local pathway choice, personalizes CTA copy/notes, and renders pathway cards on the main public entry surfaces.
- **Related-content rails** (`assets/related-content.js`, homepage/membership/VaultSparked/join/invite plus the key game/universe pages above) — Session 74 added the first shared “continue through the vault” rails; Session 79 extended that graph onto the main FORGE/lore surfaces so discovery compounds from game/lore pages instead of resetting once the user leaves the homepage or membership routes.
- **Telemetry / trust / network layer** (`assets/telemetry-matrix.js`, `assets/trust-depth.js`, `assets/network-spine.js`, `index.html`, `membership/index.html`, `vaultsparked/index.html`, `studio-pulse/index.html`) — Session 75 added the shared conversion-read, trust-depth, and ecosystem-spine modules. ## 2026-08-18 — S319 shipped behaviour changes

- **Sign-in is currently unavailable.** `/login` returns HTTP 500 (Cloudflare 1101, unhandled Worker exception) because the upstream Obelisk discovery document answers 200 with HTML. Our handler now degrades to an honest 503 carrying `identity_provider_unavailable` instead of crashing, but that guard is not yet deployed — it lives in `cloudflare/**` and the Worker lane is gated behind the same outage. Every non-identity surface is unaffected.
- **The Desk publishes on a schedule.** `news-publish.yml` drafts and publishes four editions daily with no human in the loop, authored on free self-hosted inference that fills voice and never fact, and gated by the existing editorial checks. A failed edition is dropped for that slot and retried at the next one.
- **The Desk appears on the homepage** for the first time — a server-rendered latest-edition module whose cadence line is read from the freshness receipt rather than asserted. It currently reads "Paused", which is true: the last edition is 2026-08-11.
- **The production hold is now resolved rather than binary.** It still holds; what changed is that it declares which surfaces it blocks, and a promotion provably disjoint from those surfaces can proceed while the held surfaces stay held and are named publicly.
- **The release candidate is reproducible.** Its Merkle root now covers only commit-derived bytes, so hourly telemetry can no longer invalidate a promotion candidate faster than a release can be assembled.
- Production still serves baseline `9527f227` (731 commits / 13.2 days behind). Staging serves the verified candidate `2f29768322`.

## 2026-08-18 — S319 production deploy addendum

- **Telemetry ingestion is repaired.** `/v/rum` returned HTTP 500 to every beacon POST; it now returns 202. The cause was a KV write-quota exhaustion — the limiter wrote a counter on every request against a ~1,000/day free-tier budget — and the counter is now sampled so ordinary traffic cannot exhaust it. This is why no RUM history had ever accrued and why every Desk reader-engagement figure read "unavailable".
- **Sign-in no longer crashes.** `/login` returned a bare HTTP 500 (Cloudflare 1101, unhandled Worker exception). It now returns a named 503 `auth_store_unavailable` while the daily quota is exhausted, and recovers automatically at the 00:00 UTC reset.
- Production Worker deployed and liveness-verified; staging Worker deployed and verified first.
- Static content promotion was not run this session, so production markup still serves baseline `9527f227`.


## 2026-08-20 — S324 shipped behaviour changes

- **Three public feeds that had gone stale on the live site are current again, and the reason they could go stale is closed.** `api/changelog-narrative.json` (the plain-English public changelog) was missing the newest shipped work; `api/intent-map.json` (the CANON-048 machine-readable outcome→route→evidence map agents read) had drifted; `data/stats-surface.json` and `stats.json` (the CANON-054 public stats surface) had drifted. Each was guarded by a `--check` gate that was failing — and that no runner in the repo ever invoked. The headline verification number read 319/319 green throughout.
- **A `--check` gate can no longer be born unreachable.** `scripts/check-build-gate-reachability.mjs` resolves the runner graph from `npm run build:check` (direct wiring, one-hop `STEPS`/`ADVISORY_STEPS` tables, and argv-inheriting ESM imports) and fails when any `build-*.mjs --check` has no path to it. A `--check` that is genuinely a report-only dry-run is exempt by declaring `@check-mode dry-run` in its own source, so the exemption travels with the script instead of rotting in an allowlist. 79/79 gates reachable, 3 declared dry-runs.
- **The cross-repo release handshake can hold a release again.** `build-release-dependencies --check` printed `state: rejected` and exited 0 — a well-formed rejection was a pass. It now exits 1 on `rejected` (`pending` stays non-blocking, since an unanswered-but-in-flight cargo is an honest state). It runs in the advisory lane rather than the blocking one, deliberately: the standing rejection is `obelisk-staging-registration:missing`, an unanswered Ark cargo owned by a sibling repo that CANON-018 says to resolve upstream. The rejection is now visible and named on every build instead of printed as success.
- **`build-tt-summary --check` compares something.** It derived the fresh payload and then asserted only that the committed file parsed as JSON. It now compares the control structure minus the wall-clock timestamp, the same pattern `build-security-posture` uses.
- **Six publisher crons no longer strand the feeds they feed.** Modeling the newly-gated generators in `config/evidence-graph.json` made `check-publish-cascade-coverage` visible to them, and it immediately found that `refresh-live-data`, `ci-status-beacon`, `rum-pull`, `uptime-probe`, `vault-narrative`, `news-publish`, and `cloudflare-analytics-pull` each committed a source feed without re-deriving its consumer. That is the root cause of the stale public changelog, not a one-off. All 29 workflows now report closed cascades.
- **The evidence graph models surfaces with more than one writer.** `index.html` carries server-rendered fragments from both `build-home-desk-module` and `build-launch-age`; the graph could only represent one of them, and its topological ordering silently dropped the other. Shared outputs are now declared (`sharedOutput: true`, required of every writer), edges resolve through a multimap, and a consumer waits for the last writer rather than the first.
- **No visitor-facing markup changed.** This session repaired what the public feeds report and closed the mechanism that let them drift; the pages themselves are untouched. `npm run build:check` passes 327/327 with a directly captured exit code.
- **S330 (2026-08-27) — Visitor attention is coordinated in the release candidate, but production promotion remains correctly held.** The site now has one shared `VSAttention` arbitration layer across cookie consent, install promotion, exit intent, visit-depth prompts, journey tour/feedback, returning-visitor messaging, and member-portal notices. New visitors reserve the sole automatic surface for consent; engaged returning visitors receive at most one automatic surface per tab; install/exit/depth prompts require consent and use 30-day cooldowns; portal onboarding wins before informational notices. The redundant returning-member nudge was removed and the homepage digest became an inline quiet signal. Mobile footer dispatch containment and the membership interview's 44px skip target were repaired during rendered-pixel review. Enforcement is structural (`check-attention-surface-contract` 15/15) and behavioral (`attention-surfaces.spec.js` 15/15 on exact Hetzner staging across Chromium/Firefox/WebKit); the final tree also passed `build:check` 370/370, mobile runtime 235/235 with zero P0/P1, 42/42 manually reviewed theme/viewport captures, release ceremony 8/8, and a zero-finding staged secret scan. Commits through `9eaa10424` are on `main`; scheduled sitemap commit `b70642883` is the current remote tip. **Production truth:** the full promotion gate rejects with `real-provider-e2e-pending`; required probes confirm `obelisk.identity.verify` is missing `OBELISK_RP_ID`, `OBELISK_RP_NAME`, and `OBELISK_RP_ORIGIN`, and the `obelisk-staging-registration` dependency remains missing. The live production audit therefore still exercises the older bundle and passes only 3/15 attention cases (the recent-prompt cooldown); production was not mutated or claimed current.
- **S331 (2026-08-27) — The sitewide follow-up closed four verified defects and made visitor-attention safety a release invariant.** The canonical staging ceremony now has ten steps and cannot pass unless five new/returning visitor histories succeed across Chromium, Firefox, and WebKit: exactly 15 cases, zero skips, failures, retries/flakes, or origin drift. Its aggregate public-safe receipt is bound into ceremony evidence and Doctor candidate handling. The link court now understands edge routes, template hrefs, and NDJSON, while real defects were repaired: Solara destinations resolve and VaultFront, Scriptorium, and Seamline expose their canonical live-product calls to action. Final link evidence covers 200 files and 24,361 links with zero suspect, dead-internal, or canonical-gap findings. RUM analysis now follows bounded one-hop helper arguments; that removed the false `studio-dispatch:subscribe` warning and exposed two real emitted-but-unallowlisted events, now fixed. Rendered review covered 42 desktop/mobile/theme states with zero defects; mobile runtime passed 235/235; the full build gate passed 370/370; exact staging attention passed 15/15; and the release ceremony passed 10/10. Production was not mutated: `real-provider-e2e-pending`, missing Obelisk relying-party values, and missing staging registration remain the honest full-site promotion hold.
- **S332 (2026-08-28) — Attention evidence, destination reachability, and evidence-age truth are fully deployed and live.** Automatic surfaces emit only fixed-vocabulary, identifier-free `attention:claimed` events after consent; the public rollup groups by automatic surface and coarse visit depth and suppresses every group below 20 claims. CTA readiness now names whether evidence is current, aging, stale, or absent instead of presenting an old countdown as current. Canonical product reachability samples at most 12 URLs derived from the sanitized public registry, requires two 404/410 observations to fail, and publishes transient/access-limited outcomes as unknown (10 pass, 0 fail, 2 unknown). Refresh Live Data now calls the News Desk with its required `--rebuild` mode, and the served-surface, smoke, accessibility, sitemap, closeout-boundary, and cross-browser attention regressions found during CI/release were root-fixed without weakening their gates. Cloudflare Pages run `33167403022` deployed static SHA `d2b15bcf90b6e82e2e9840933b99bdabdd065512`; Worker run `33167667067` deployed successfully. The apex serves `/`, `/status/`, `/ask-founders/`, `/api/agent-actions/v1`, the attention and destination feeds, the candidate manifest, and build provenance with CSP; the live attention suite passes **15/15** across Chromium, Firefox, and WebKit.
